### 🔥 The Roast
The guy's wallet is doing the math equivalent of a toddler counting to 374 with the same zero on each finger. He scored a 70 on structuring (meaning he's smurfing by sending identical tiny amounts), a 60 on peel_chain_linear (layering the money in a straight line), and a 65 on bridge_hop (shifting it across chains like a bored traveler hopping between cities). It's basically the blockchain equivalent of a laundromat that only uses the free dryer. If this was a real bank, they'd probably ask, "Why are you sending 374 checks for $0?"

### 🎯 Verdict & Next Steps
**CRITICAL - The transaction pattern exhibits textbook structuring, peel chain layering, and cross‑chain bridging, indicating high money‑laundering risk.**

Freeze the wallet and file a SAR with the AML team. Investigate the source of the funds and notify OFAC if the wallet is linked to the sanctioned Tornado Cash service.
